await (async(page)=>{
 await page.bringToFront();await page.waitForFunction(()=>typeof window.atlasCamera==='function');
 const environment=await page.evaluate(()=>{const g=document.querySelector('canvas').getContext('webgl2'),e=g.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');return {url:location.href,viewport:[innerWidth,innerHeight],dpr:devicePixelRatio,renderDpr:window.atlasInspection().viewport.dpr,camera:window.atlasCamera(),renderer:g.getParameter(e.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL)};});
 if(Math.abs(environment.camera.yaw)>1)throw new Error('Expected yaw zero');
 const trials=[];
 const snapshot=()=>page.evaluate(()=>({character:window.atlasCharacter.snapshot(),inspection:window.atlasInspection()}));
 const move=async(keys,ms,run)=>{try{if(run)await page.keyboard.down('Shift');for(const key of keys)await page.keyboard.down(key);await page.waitForTimeout(ms);}finally{for(const key of keys)await page.keyboard.up(key);await page.keyboard.up('Shift');}await page.waitForTimeout(120);return snapshot();};
 for(const run of [false,true])for(const [label,keys,reverse,walkMs]of [
 ['north',['KeyW'],['KeyS'],1200],['west',['KeyA'],['KeyD'],900],
 ['northwest',['KeyW','KeyA'],['KeyS','KeyD'],1100],['northeast',['KeyW','KeyD'],['KeyS','KeyA'],600]]){
 await page.evaluate(async()=>{const pc=await import(performance.getEntriesByType('resource').find(e=>e.name.includes('/deps/playcanvas.js')).name),p=pc.Application.getApplication().root.findByName('Player');p.rigidbody.teleport(20.45954132080078,1.6,34.41954803466797);p.rigidbody.linearVelocity=new pc.Vec3();});await page.waitForTimeout(700);
 const start=await snapshot(),ms=walkMs/(run?1.8:1),out=await move(keys,ms,run),back=await move(reverse,ms,run),exit=await move(['KeyW'],1200,false);
 const pos=s=>s.character.avatar;
 const distance=Math.hypot(pos(out)[0]-pos(start)[0],pos(out)[2]-pos(start)[2]);
 const returnError=Math.hypot(pos(back)[0]-pos(start)[0],pos(back)[2]-pos(start)[2]);
 const exitProgress=pos(back)[2]-pos(exit)[2];
 const result={label,run,ms,start,out,back,exit,distance,returnError,exitProgress};trials.push(result);
 if(distance<walkMs*.0015||returnError>.5||exitProgress<1.8||[out,back,exit].some(s=>pos(s)[1]<.3||s.inspection.recoveries!==start.inspection.recoveries))throw new Error(JSON.stringify(trials));
 }
 await page.evaluate(async()=>{const pc=await import(performance.getEntriesByType('resource').find(e=>e.name.includes('/deps/playcanvas.js')).name),p=pc.Application.getApplication().root.findByName('Player');p.rigidbody.teleport(20.45954132080078,1.6,34.41954803466797);p.rigidbody.linearVelocity=new pc.Vec3();});await page.waitForTimeout(700);
 const final=await snapshot();await page.screenshot({path:'output/playwright/steps/pit-2-fixed.png'});
 return {environment,pages:page.context().pages().length,trials,final};
})(page);
